# Development status

ROADMAP.md is authoritative. Snapshot: 2026-10-06, America/Toronto. Latest scoped owner approval: `cd0c991a18909459f27eb6abd10843963345b748`; see [development-review.md](development-review.md).

## Repository

Authenticated access verified the intended GitHub repository is private and empty. Local Git was initialized on `main` with the supplied URL as `origin`; no content has been pushed. The original roadmap was preserved.

declared records into document/row evidence and common normalized metrics. Exact decimal literals, units, periods, geography, model assumptions, uncertainty and multiline notes are retained; unknown cells stay null, ambiguous/nonfinite/extreme quantities and identity/path/schema mismatches block the whole file. [The contract](business-ingestion-contract.md) and [raw-source workflow evidence](../evaluations/sus23-business-source-workflow.md) connect the exact imported candidate to the unchanged manager CLI, including the explicit separate-factor and absent-factor cases.
